Following this incident, eMudhra announced its exit from the publicly trusted TLS certificate business and is undergoing an independent review to improve its key-generation procedures.

A particularly dangerous Linux botnet known as Kinsing has been gaining attention due to its capabilities for remote command execution, mass-scanning, and worm-like infection that exploits weak Redis passwords and SSH keys. It is primarily used to mine Monero cryptocurrency and can spread rapidly across servers, posing a significant threat to internet-facing Linux systems without proper security measures.

 - Kinsing is a Go-written bot that allows for remote command execution and operates through various malicious scripts.
 - The bot utilizes weak Redis authentication and SSH key exploits to facilitate its spread across heavily impacted servers.
 - Defense measures include ensuring strong authentication on Redis and SSH, as well as monitoring for unusual activity and process behavior.

Source: https://blog.malwaremustdie.org/2026/11/mmd-071-2026-kinsingbot-well-designed.html

The Jones Act, a maritime law in the U.S., mandates that goods shipped by water must be transported on U.S.-built, crewed, and owned vessels. A waiver currently in effect allows for greater flexibility in shipping certain goods to address economic and logistical challenges, particularly in the energy and agriculture sectors, but is set to expire soon. There are ongoing discussions regarding the future of this waiver and potential changes to the law to improve domestic shipping efficiency.

 - The Jones Act requires goods shipped in the U.S. to be transported on domestically constructed vessels.
 - A current waiver allows for increased shipments of energy and agricultural products, impacting economic security.

In 2025, nearly 60% of social welfare benefits for families with children in Germany were allocated to foreign recipients, with Ukrainian nationals receiving the largest share. The total welfare expenditure on foreigners has significantly increased, raising questions about the overall impact on the German social welfare system and contributing to a trend of growing numbers of naturalized citizens among those receiving assistance.

 - In 2025, €12.2 billion in welfare for families with kids was paid to foreign recipients in Germany.
 - The share of social welfare recipients who are foreigners has increased from 19.6% in 2010 to 47.3% in 2025.

Source: https://www.zerohedge.com/geopolitical/german-taxpayers-bled-dry-nearly-60-welfare-families-kids-goes-foreigners
